Path('/root/Aurora_Rights_Rollforward_4-25.xlsx')\n\nENTITY_NAME = 'Aurora Stream'\n\nHEADERS = [\n    'Vendor',\n    'Beginning Balance',\n    'Jan Adds', 'Jan Amortization', 'Jan Ending Balance',\n    'Feb Adds', 'Feb Amortization', 'Feb Ending Balance',\n    'Mar Adds', 'Mar Amortization', 'Mar Ending Balance',\n    'Apr Adds', 'Apr Amortization', 'Apr Ending Balance',\n    'Apr Ending (Link)'  # Column O: the link/summary feed column\n]\n\nCSV_TO_COL = {\n    'vendor': 'A',\n    'beginning_balance': 'B',\n    'jan_adds': 'C',\n    'jan_amortization': 'D',\n    'jan_ending_balance': 'E',\n    'feb_adds': 'F',\n    'feb_amortization': 'G',\n    'feb_ending_balance': 'H',\n    'mar_adds': 'I',\n    'mar_amortization': 'J',\n    'mar_ending_balance': 'K',\n    'apr_adds': 'L',\n    'apr_amortization': 'M',\n    'apr_ending_balance': 'N',\n}\n\nNUM_FIELDS = {\n    'beginning_balance',\n    'jan_adds','jan_amortization','jan_ending_balance',\n    'feb_adds','feb_amortization','feb_ending_balance',\n    'mar_adds','mar_amortization','mar_ending_balance',\n    'apr_adds','apr_amortization','apr_ending_balance',\n}\n\n\ndef load_schedule(path: Path):\n    with path.open('r', newline='') as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        rows = []\n        for r in reader:\n            # Skip completely empty lines\n            if not r or all((v is None or str(v).strip()=='') for v in r.values()):\n                continue\n            clean = {}\n            for k, v in r.items():\n                if v is None:\n                    clean[k] = None\n                    continue\n                v = v.strip()\n                if k in NUM_FIELDS:\n                    clean[k] = float(v) if v != '' else 0.0\n                else:\n                    clean[k] = v\n            rows.append(clean)\n        return rows\n\n\nledger = json.loads(LEDGER_JSON.read_text())\nfilm_rows = load_schedule(FILM_CSV)\nmusic_rows = load_schedule(MUSIC_CSV)\n\n# Build workbook\nwb = Workbook()\n# Remove default sheet\nwb.remove(wb.active)\n\nws_summary = wb.create_sheet('Rights Summary')\nws_film = wb.create_sheet('Film Rights #2710')\nws_music = wb.create_sheet('Music Rights #2720')\n\n\ndef write_detail(ws, account_name: str, schedule_rows, gl):\n    # Title area\n    ws['A1'] = ENTITY_NAME\n    ws['A2'] = account_name\n\n    # Column headers on row 5\n    for idx, h in enumerate(HEADERS, start=1):\n        ws.cell(row=5, column=idx, value=h)\n\n    start_row = 6\n    for i, r in enumerate(schedule_rows):\n        rr = start_row + i\n        # Vendor\n        ws[f\"A{rr}\"] = r['vendor']\n        # Numeric schedule columns\n        for field, col in CSV_TO_COL.items():\n            if field == 'vendor':\n                continue\n            ws[f\"{col}{rr}\"] = r[field]\n        # Column O (15): summary-link column; store numeric (not string)\n        ws[f\"O{rr}\"] = float(r['apr_ending_balance'])\n\n    last_item_row = start_row + len(schedule_rows) - 1\n\n    # Control rows\n    row_month_totals = last_item_row + 1\n    row_ending = row_month_totals + 1\n    row_variance = row_month_totals + 2\n    row_gl = row_month_totals + 3\n\n    ws[f\"A{row_month_totals}\"] = 'Month Totals'\n    ws[f\"A{row_ending}\"] = 'Ending Balance'\n    ws[f\"A{row_variance}\"] = 'Variance'\n    ws[f\"A{row_gl}\"] = 'GL Balance'\n\n    # Month Totals = SUM of line items for each numeric column B:O\n    for col in range(2, 16):\n        letter = get_column_letter(col)\n        ws[f\"{letter}{row_month_totals}\"] = f\"=SUM({letter}{start_row}:{letter}{last_item_row})\"\n\n    # Ending Balance row\n    # Carry totals for beginning/adds/amort columns; compute ending columns via rollforward\n    # Beginning total\n    ws[f\"B{row_ending}\"] = f\"=B{row_month_totals}\"\n    # Adds/amort totals\n    for letter in list('CDFGIJLM'):\n        ws[f\"{letter}{row_ending}\"] = f\"={letter}{row_month_totals}\"\n\n    # Ending balances by month\n    ws[f\"E{row_ending}\"] = f\"=B{row_ending}+C{row_ending}-D{row_ending}\"\n    ws[f\"H{row_ending}\"] = f\"=E{row_ending}+F{row_ending}-G{row_ending}\"\n    ws[f\"K{row_ending}\"] = f\"=H{row_ending}+I{row_ending}-J{row_ending}\"\n    ws[f\"N{row_ending}\"] = f\"=K{row_ending}+L{row_ending}-M{row_ending}\"\n    ws[f\"O{row_ending}\"] = f\"=N{row_ending}\"\n\n    # GL Balance row (populate month ending columns; link total O to Apr)\n    ws[f\"E{row_gl}\"] = float(gl['jan'])\n    ws[f\"H{row_gl}\"] = float(gl['feb'])\n    ws[f\"K{row_gl}\"] = float(gl['mar'])\n    ws[f\"N{row_gl}\"] = float(gl['apr'])\n    ws[f\"O{row_gl}\"] = f\"=N{row_gl}\"\n\n    # Variance row = Ending - GL for ending columns\n    for letter in ['E','H','K','N','O']:\n        ws[f\"{letter}{row_variance}\"] = f\"={letter}{row_ending}-{letter}{row_gl}\"\n\n    return {\n        'start_row': start_row,\n        'last_item_row': last_item_row,\n        'row_month_totals': row_month_totals,\n        'row_ending': row_ending,\n        'row_variance': row_variance,\n        'row_gl': row_gl,\n    }\n\nfilm_rows_info = write_detail(ws_film, 'Film Rights #2710', film_rows, ledger['film_rights_2710'])\nmusic_rows_info = write_detail(ws_music, 'Music Rights #2720', #2720'!O{music_rows_info['row_gl']}\"\nws_summary['B14'] = f\"='Music Rights #2720'!O{music_rows_info['row_variance']}\"\n\nws_summary['A16'] = 'Total Variance'\nws_summary['B16'] = '=B9+B14'\n\n# Step 6: print key info before saving\nprint('Sheet order:', wb.sheetnames)\nprint('A1 values:', {name: wb[name]['A1'].value for name in wb.sheetnames})\n\nfor ws, info in [(ws_film, film_rows_info),
